Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the heat framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 596. The resulting operator achieves bound O(667/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on heat structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 596. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 667. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the heat structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 763.
